A pair of affordable crew socks designed by a Highland heritage maker, made of acrylic yarn in black and red tartan, machine-knit on a high-speed circular machine. They rise to mid-calf with a ribbed leg. Their signature detail is a silver clan-style badge, printed on with sublimation dye. They have a plain elastic cuff. Practical details include a seamless toe and odor-resistant fibers.
A pair of sturdy hiking socks designed by a Celtic knotwork artisan, made of recycled cotton blend in oxblood and bronze, machine-knit with a machine-linked toe. They are cushioned crew socks with a thick terry-loop sole. They feature braided trim, embroidered by machine. They have a comfort-ribbed cuff and reinforced heel and toe. They balance moisture-wicking fibers with the whimsy of a knitted-in glow-in-the-dark moon at the ankle.

A pair of luxurious athletic quarter socks designed by a Suzhou silk embroiderer, made of qiviut musk-ox down in black with gold, hand-knit on fine double-pointed needles. They rise just above the ankle with a cushioned footbed. Their signature detail is cloud-collar patterning, hand-embroidered in silk and metallic thread. They have a hand-turned picot-edged cuff. Alongside a cushioned sole, they add a different mismatched pattern on each sock that forms one picture side by side for playfulness. They are finished with a fine line of hand-sewn crystal beads at the cuff.

They have a non-binding comfort cuff and reinforced heel and toe. They balance moisture-wicking fibers with the whimsy of a pair of small embroidered bees at the ankle.
A pair of beautifully finished toe socks designed by a Rococo couturier, made of baby alpaca in lavender and cream, knit at 240 needles and hand-linked at the toe. They have five individually knit toes. Their signature detail is delicate lace-like patterning, hand-embroidered in silk thread. They have a hand-turned, non-binding cuff and reinforced heel and toe. Practical details include an arch-support band and a seamless toe.
A pair of exquisite over-the-knee socks designed by a Venetian carnival mask-maker, made of pure mulberry silk in teal and gold, hand-knit on fine double-pointed needles. They rise just above the knee. They feature jeweled filigree overlays, hand-embroidered in silk and metallic thread. They have a hand-turned picot-edged cuff. Alongside odor-resistant fibers, they add toes striped in a different color on every toe for playfulness. They are finished with a hand-embroidered silk monogram at the cuff.

Alongside reinforced high-wear zones, they add a different mismatched pattern on each sock that forms one picture side by side for playfulness. They are finished with a band of tiny hand-sewn natural pearls at the cuff.

Practical details include an arch-support band and mesh ventilation panels.

They balance reinforced high-wear zones with the whimsy of a pair of small embroidered bees at the ankle.

Alongside a seamless toe, they add a pair of small embroidered bees at the ankle for playfulness.
A pair of polished athletic quarter socks designed by an oceanic designer, made of baby alpaca in deep navy and foam white, knit slowly on vintage machines and hand-linked at the toe. They rise just above the ankle with a cushioned footbed. Their signature detail is wave-shaped ridges, knit in as hand-guided intarsia. They have a hand-turned, non-binding cuff and reinforced heel and toe. Practical details include a seamless toe and an arch-support band.
A pair of luxurious split-toe tabi socks designed by a royal-court craftsman from Rajasthan, made of pure Mongolian cashmere in ruby red and silver, hand-knit on fine double-pointed needles. They have a split between the big toe and the others. They feature scattered mirror-work, hand-embroidered in silk and metallic thread. They have a hand-turned picot-edged cuff. Whimsical touches include tiny embroidered stars that spell a secret word along the cuff and a knitted-in glow-in-the-dark moon at the ankle. They are finished with a hand-embroidered silk monogram at the cuff.

They have a hand-turned, non-binding cuff and reinforced heel and toe. They balance a seamless toe with the whimsy of a tiny knitted cat's face on each toe.
A pair of luxurious knee-high socks designed by a steampunk inventor, made of qiviut musk-ox down in black and aged bronze, hand-knit on fine double-pointed needles. They rise to just below the knee. Their signature detail is copper rivets, hand-embroidered in silk and metallic thread. They have a hand-turned picot-edged cuff. They balance reinforced high-wear zones with the whimsy of a knitted-in hidden message on the sole. They are finished with a fine line of hand-sewn crystal beads at the cuff.
A pair of one-of-a-kind no-show socks designed by an Ottoman-inspired court artisan, made of hand-spun vicuña and silk in forest green and gold, hand-knit by a master over many weeks. They are cut very low to hide inside shoes. They feature tulip and carnation motifs, knit in by hand with gold-wrapped thread. They have a heel lined in kid suede. Practical details include moisture-wicking fibers and an arch-support band. They are finished with a band of tiny hand-sewn natural pearls at the cuff.

Alongside a padded shin panel, they add tiny knitted ears at the back of each cuff for playfulness. They are finished with a band of tiny hand-sewn natural pearls at the cuff.
